1. EXACT FROZEN ROOT

def QuasiFeffermanBStatement : Prop :=
  ∀ (ν : ℝ), 0 < ν → ∀ (a : CriticalPhysicalL2), EnergySigmaZero a →
    (∃ (u : Velocity) (G : WeakGradientTrajectory), CriticalLerayHopf ν a u G) ∧
    ∀ (u : Velocity) (G : WeakGradientTrajectory), CriticalLerayHopf ν a u G →
      HasSmoothTail ν u (safeCutoff a ν) ∧
      ∀ (δ : ℝ), cutoff a ν < δ →
        HasSmoothTail ν u δ ∧ TailEnergyEquality ν u G δ ∧ UniqueFuture ν u G δ


ROOT_SOURCE_SHA256 = 9229c7f8ece4d6adcbe27792b8d34b649f58fb9a296402f916c71f689e523512

theorem QUASI_FEFFERMAN_B_COMPOSED : QuasiFeffermanBStatement :=
  Quasi_Fefferman_B_of_LH_existence Q01_LERAY_HOPF_EXISTENCE

end QuasiFeffermanB

cutoff(a,nu) = ||a||_2^4 / (8*pi^2*nu^5)
safeCutoff(a,nu) = 2 * cutoff(a,nu)
ROOT_EXISTENCE_WITNESS = independent of the universally quantified original u,G
TAIL = closed [delta,infinity), every delta > cutoff
ORIGINAL_GRADIENT = G; identification a.e. in time
ZERO_DATUM = closed safe cutoff 0 included
ANALYTIC_PREMISE_MUTATIONS = 0

TYPE_BEGIN
QuasiFeffermanB.CALORIC_H1_GALERKIN_ENSTROPHY_ACCUMULATION : ∀ (ν : ℝ),
  0 < ν →
    ∀ (a : SingularClosure.CriticalSobolev 2),
      SingularClosure.SpectralReality (SingularClosure.criticalCoeff 2 a) →
        ∀ (S : ℝ),
          (∀ (N : ℕ)
              (g :
                SingularClosure.GalerkinSolution ν
                  (SingularClosure.finiteSynthesis N (SingularClosure.criticalCoeff 2 a)) N)
              (T : ℝ),
              0 ≤ T →
                ∫ (t : ℝ) in 0..T, QuasiFeffermanB.finiteChi0 (SingularClosure.cutoffModes N) (g.coeff t) ^ 2 ≤ S) →
            ∀ (T : ℝ),
              0 ≤ T →
                ∀ (N : ℕ)
                  (g :
                    SingularClosure.GalerkinSolution ν
                      (SingularClosure.finiteSynthesis N (SingularClosure.criticalCoeff 2 a)) N),
                  ∀ t ∈ Set.Icc 0 T,
                    SingularClosure.coefficientDissipation N (g.coeff t) +
                        ν * ∫ (s : ℝ) in 0..t, SingularClosure.coefficientLaplacianSquare N (g.coeff s) ≤
                      ‖a‖ ^ 2 * Real.exp (S / ν)
TYPE_END

  0 < ν →
    ∀ (b : SingularClosure.CriticalSobolev 2),
      SingularClosure.SpectralReality (SingularClosure.criticalCoeff 2 b) →
        SingularClosure.SpectralSolenoidal (SingularClosure.criticalCoeff 2 b) →
          SingularClosure.SpectralMeanZero (SingularClosure.criticalCoeff 2 b) →
            ∀ (S : ℝ),
              (∀ (N : ℕ)
                  (g :
                    SingularClosure.GalerkinSolution ν
                      (SingularClosure.finiteSynthesis N (SingularClosure.criticalCoeff 2 b)) N)
                  (T : ℝ),
                  0 ≤ T →
                    ∫ (t : ℝ) in 0..T, QuasiFeffermanB.finiteChi0 (SingularClosure.cutoffModes N) (g.coeff t) ^ 2 ≤ S) →
                ∃ L,
                  SingularClosure.PositiveSmoothNSE ν L.velocity L.pressure ∧
                    Filter.Tendsto
                        (fun t =>
                          MeasureTheory.eLpNorm
                            (L.velocity t -
                              ↑↑(SingularClosure.criticalInitialPhysicalL2
                                    ((SingularClosure.criticalSobolevLower 1 2 ⋯) b)))
                            2 (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict SingularClosure.unitCube))
                        (nhdsWithin 0 (Set.Ioi 0)) (nhds 0) ∧
                      (∀ (T : ℝ),
                          0 < T →
                            MeasureTheory.MemLp (fun t => SingularClosure.criticalSpatialNorm 6 (L.velocity t)) 4
                              (MeasureTheory.volume.restrict (Set.Ioc 0 T))) ∧
                        (∀ (t : ℝ),
                            0 < t → SingularClosure.physicalDissipation (L.velocity t) ≤ ‖b‖ ^ 2 * Real.exp (S / ν)) ∧
                          ∀ (v : SingularClosure.Velocity)
                            (G : ℝ → SingularClosure.Vec → SingularClosure.Vec →L[ℝ] SingularClosure.Vec),
                            SingularClosure.CriticalLerayHopf ν
                                (SingularClosure.criticalInitialPhysicalL2
                                  ((SingularClosure.criticalSobolevLower 1 2 ⋯) b))
                                v G →
                              ∀ (t : ℝ),
                                0 < t → v t =ᵐ[MeasureTheory.volume.restrict SingularClosure.unitCube] L.velocity t
TYPE_END

!=! QuasiFeffermanB.COEFFICIENT_TRANSPORT_SHARP_EUCLIDEAN

QuasiFeffermanB.COEFFICIENT_TRANSPORT_SHARP_EUCLIDEAN : ∀ (N : ℕ) (c : SingularClosure.FourierCoeff),
  (∀ p ∉ SingularClosure.cutoffModes N, ∀ (i : Fin 3), c p i = 0) →
    SingularClosure.SpectralSolenoidal c →
      ∀ (k : SingularClosure.Frequency),
        k ≠ 0 →
          QuasiFeffermanB.complexVecNorm
              (SingularClosure.coefficientLeray k (SingularClosure.coefficientTransport N c k)) ≤
            SingularClosure.criticalFrequencyWeight k / 2 *
              ∑ p ∈ SingularClosure.cutoffModes N,
                QuasiFeffermanB.complexVecNorm (c p) * QuasiFeffermanB.complexVecNorm (c (k - p))
TYPE_END

!=! QuasiFeffermanB.ENERGY_FINITE_GALERKIN_WEAK_PAIRING

SOURCE_SHA256 = 4bd186ac162da2bd62b36a632754b75f6859d9057d9b24f8079c852b22f4a028

TYPE_BEGIN
QuasiFeffermanB.ENERGY_FINITE_GALERKIN_WEAK_PAIRING : ∀ (ν : ℝ) (N : ℕ) (c b : SingularClosure.FourierCoeff),
  (∀ k ∉ SingularClosure.cutoffModes N, ∀ (i : Fin 3), c k i = 0) →
    SingularClosure.SpectralReality c →
      SingularClosure.SpectralSolenoidal c →
        SingularClosure.SpectralReality b →
          SingularClosure.SpectralSolenoidal b →
            SingularClosure.physicalFieldInner (SingularClosure.finiteSynthesis N (SingularClosure.galerkinRhs ν N c))
                (SingularClosure.finiteSynthesis N b) =
              ν *
                  SingularClosure.physicalFieldInner (SingularClosure.finiteSynthesis N c)
                    (SingularClosure.laplacian (SingularClosure.finiteSynthesis N b)) +
                SingularClosure.criticalWeakTransport (SingularClosure.finiteSynthesis N c)
                  (SingularClosure.finiteSynthesis N b)
TYPE_END

      (R : ℝ),

        ∀ (U : ℝ → SingularClosure.TorusSobolev 0),
          (∀ (k : SingularClosure.Frequency) (i : Fin 3), ContinuousOn (fun t => ↑(U t) k i) (Set.Ici 0)) →
            (∀ (r : ℝ),
                0 ≤ r →

                      Filter.Tendsto (fun n => (g n).energyTimeH0L2 T) Filter.atTop (nhds z) ∧

                          2 * ν *
                            ∫ (r : ℝ) in 0..t,

                        R ^ 2) ∧
                  ∀ (T : ℝ),

                        ∀ t ∈ Set.Icc s T,

                              2 * ν *
                                ∫ (r : ℝ) in s..t,
                                  SingularClosure.criticalGradientEnergy (QuasiFeffermanB.energyGlobalGradient U r) ≤
                            SingularClosure.physicalEnergy (QuasiFeffermanB.energyPeriodicVelocity U s)

!=! QuasiFeffermanB.FINITE_CALORIC_QUADRATIC_ESTIMATE
SOURCE = QuasiFeffermanB/CaloricBootstrap.lean
SOURCE_SHA256 = 64ea44a78ade4a6ef701c7a023bc2eebefecd1dfe6cb30f9773a1e80a209a770
AXIOMS = ["Classical.choice", "Quot.sound", "propext"]
TYPE_BEGIN
@QuasiFeffermanB.FINITE_CALORIC_QUADRATIC_ESTIMATE : ∀ {ι : Type u_1} (s : Finset ι) (κ : ι → ℝ) (ν T H : ℝ),
  0 < ν →
    0 ≤ T →
      (∀ i ∈ s, 0 < κ i) →
        ∀ (A h : ℝ → ℝ) (f : ι → ℝ → ℝ),
          ContinuousOn A (Set.Icc 0 T) →
            ContinuousOn h (Set.Icc 0 T) →
              (∀ i ∈ s, ContinuousOn (f i) (Set.Icc 0 T)) →
                (∀ t ∈ Set.Icc 0 T, 0 ≤ A t) →
                  (∀ t ∈ Set.Icc 0 T, 0 ≤ h t) →
                    (∀ i ∈ s, ∀ t ∈ Set.Icc 0 T, 0 ≤ f i t) →
                      (∀ t ∈ Set.Icc 0 T,
                          A t ≤ h t + 1 / 2 * ∑ i ∈ s, QuasiFeffermanB.caloricVolterra ν (κ i) (f i) t) →
                        (∀ t ∈ Set.Icc 0 T, ∑ i ∈ s, f i t ≤ A t ^ 2) →
                          QuasiFeffermanB.finiteTimeL2 T h ≤ H →
                            QuasiFeffermanB.finiteTimeL2 T A ≤
                              H + 1 / (2 * √(2 * ν)) * QuasiFeffermanB.finiteTimeL2 T A ^ 2
TYPE_END

!=! QuasiFeffermanB.FINITE_WEIGHTED_SCHUR

TYPE_BEGIN
@QuasiFeffermanB.FINITE_WEIGHTED_SCHUR : ∀ {ι : Type u_1} (s : Finset ι) (M : ι → ι → ℝ) (w x : ι → ℝ) (C : ℝ),
  (∀ i ∈ s, ∀ j ∈ s, 0 ≤ M i j) →
    (∀ i ∈ s, ∀ j ∈ s, M i j = M j i) →
      (∀ i ∈ s, 0 < w i) →
        (∀ i ∈ s, ∑ j ∈ s, M i j * w j ≤ C * w i) → ∑ i ∈ s, ∑ j ∈ s, M i j * x i * x j ≤ C * ∑ i ∈ s, x i ^ 2
TYPE_END

  (∀ i ∈ s, ∀ j ∈ s, 0 ≤ M i j) →
    (∀ i ∈ s, ∀ j ∈ s, M i j = M j i) →

        (∀ i ∈ s, ∑ j ∈ s, M i j * w j ≤ d i * w i) → ∑ i ∈ s, ∑ j ∈ s, M i j * x i * x j ≤ ∑ i ∈ s, d i * x i ^ 2

!=! QuasiFeffermanB.GALERKIN_CHI0_BUDGET_OF_CALORIC_QUADRATIC

  0 < ν →
    ∀ (B : ℝ),
      0 ≤ B →
        B < ν ^ 2 / 2 →
          (∀ k ∈ SingularClosure.cutoffModes N,
              ∀ (t : ℝ),
                0 ≤ t →
                  QuasiFeffermanB.complexVecNorm
                      (SingularClosure.coefficientLeray k (SingularClosure.coefficientTransport N (g.coeff t) k)) ≤
                    SingularClosure.criticalFrequencyWeight k / 2 *
                      QuasiFeffermanB.finiteModeConvolution N (g.coeff t) k) →
            (QuasiFeffermanB.finiteCaloricQuadratic (SingularClosure.cutoffModes N)
                  SingularClosure.criticalFrequencyWeight fun k => QuasiFeffermanB.complexVecNorm (g.coeff 0 k)) ≤
                B →
              ∀ (T : ℝ),
                0 ≤ T →
                  ∫ (t : ℝ) in 0..T, QuasiFeffermanB.finiteChi0 (SingularClosure.cutoffModes N) (g.coeff t) ^ 2 ≤
                    4 * B / ν

                    StrictMono φ ∧
                      ContinuousOn u (Set.Ici 0) ∧
                        u 0 = a ∧
                          (∀ (t : ℝ), 0 ≤ t → ‖u t‖ ≤ L + 1) ∧
                            ∀ (T : ℝ),
                              TendstoUniformlyOn (fun n t => (g (φ n)).sobolevState 0 t)
                                (fun t => (SingularClosure.criticalToTorusH0 1 ⋯) (u t)) Filter.atTop (Set.Icc 0 T)
TYPE_END

!=! QuasiFeffermanB.GALERKIN_MODE_DUHAMEL
SOURCE = QuasiFeffermanB/CaloricGalerkinBudget.lean
SOURCE_SHA256 = 7dc18a2500ff53777e774b4775ca9582f21cbf37836c0cb0e805ce0499702cbd
AXIOMS = ["Classical.choice", "Quot.sound", "propext"]
TYPE_BEGIN
@QuasiFeffermanB.GALERKIN_MODE_DUHAMEL : ∀ {ν : ℝ} {a : SingularClosure.Field} {N : ℕ}
  (g : SingularClosure.GalerkinSolution ν a N),
  ∀ k ∈ SingularClosure.cutoffModes N,
    ∀ (T : ℝ),
      0 ≤ T →
        QuasiFeffermanB.complexVec (g.coeff T k) =
          Real.exp (-(ν * (2 * Real.pi) ^ 2 * ↑(SingularClosure.galerkinFreqSq k)) * T) •
              QuasiFeffermanB.complexVec (g.coeff 0 k) +
            ∫ (s : ℝ) in 0..T,
              Real.exp (-(ν * (2 * Real.pi) ^ 2 * ↑(SingularClosure.galerkinFreqSq k)) * (T - s)) •
                -QuasiFeffermanB.complexVec
                    (SingularClosure.coefficientLeray k (SingularClosure.coefficientTransport N (g.coeff s) k))
TYPE_END

!=! QuasiFeffermanB.GALERKIN_MODE_DUHAMEL_NORM

SOURCE_SHA256 = 57e0265b2cb4b867977803ec1742242b8f2a1f256b4ef99c9f9e6d386da1e881
AXIOMS = ["Classical.choice", "Quot.sound", "propext"]
TYPE_BEGIN
@QuasiFeffermanB.GALERKIN_MODE_DUHAMEL_NORM : ∀ {ν : ℝ} {a : SingularClosure.Field} {N : ℕ}

  ∀ k ∈ SingularClosure.cutoffModes N,

          Real.exp (-(ν * (2 * Real.pi) ^ 2 * ↑(SingularClosure.galerkinFreqSq k)) * T) *
              QuasiFeffermanB.complexVecNorm (g.coeff 0 k) +
            ∫ (s : ℝ) in 0..T,

                QuasiFeffermanB.complexVecNorm

!=! QuasiFeffermanB.LH_RESTART_FROM_ENERGY_START

!=! QuasiFeffermanB.QUADRATIC_BARRIER_LE_TWO_HEAT_BUDGET
SOURCE = QuasiFeffermanB/CaloricBarrier.lean
SOURCE_SHA256 = 0b1f8a8d663ed43dbe0efcd742c6bc0ca4a10f42be5bbd66c00a521f44538df4
AXIOMS = ["Classical.choice", "Quot.sound", "propext"]
TYPE_BEGIN
QuasiFeffermanB.QUADRATIC_BARRIER_LE_TWO_HEAT_BUDGET : ∀ (X : ℝ → ℝ) (h b T : ℝ),
  0 ≤ h →
    0 < b →
      4 * b * h < 1 →
        ContinuousOn X (Set.Icc 0 T) →
          X 0 = 0 →
            (∀ t ∈ Set.Icc 0 T, 0 ≤ X t) → (∀ t ∈ Set.Icc 0 T, X t ≤ h + b * X t ^ 2) → ∀ t ∈ Set.Icc 0 T, X t ≤ 2 * h

@QuasiFeffermanB.REAL_SCALAR_DUHAMEL : ∀ {E : Type u_1} [inst : NormedAddCommGroup E] [inst_1 : NormedSpace ℝ E]
  [CompleteSpace E] (α T : ℝ) (u f : ℝ → E),
  0 ≤ T →
    ContinuousOn u (Set.Icc 0 T) →
      ContinuousOn f (Set.Icc 0 T) →

TYPE_END

QuasiFeffermanB.coefficientLeray_transport_symmetric : ∀ (N : ℕ) (c : SingularClosure.FourierCoeff),

@QuasiFeffermanB.criticalFourthPower_budget : ∀ {α : Type u_1} [inst : MeasurableSpace α] {m : MeasureTheory.Measure α}
  {kinetic gradient critical : α → ℝ} {M μ : ℝ},
  0 ≤ M →
    MeasureTheory.Integrable gradient m →
      MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable (fun t => critical t ^ 2) m →
        (∀ᵐ (t : α) ∂m, 0 ≤ gradient t ∧ kinetic t ≤ M ∧ critical t ^ 2 ≤ kinetic t * gradient t) →
          ∫ (t : α), gradient t ∂m ≤ M / (2 * μ) → ∫ (t : α), critical t ^ 2 ∂m ≤ M ^ 2 / (2 * μ)

    MeasureTheory.AEStronglyMeasurable (fun t => critical t ^ 2) m →
      (∀ᵐ (t : α) ∂m, 0 ≤ gradient t ∧ kinetic t ≤ M ∧ critical t ^ 2 ≤ kinetic t * gradient t) →

!=! QuasiFeffermanB.energyGalerkin_H0_time_difference_bound
SOURCE = QuasiFeffermanB/EnergyTimeCompactness.lean

AXIOMS = ["Classical.choice", "Quot.sound", "propext"]
TYPE_BEGIN
@QuasiFeffermanB.energyGalerkin_H0_time_difference_bound : ∀ {ν : ℝ} {a b : SingularClosure.Field} {N M : ℕ}
  (g : SingularClosure.GalerkinSolution ν a N) (h : SingularClosure.GalerkinSolution ν b M) (T B η : ℝ),
  0 ≤ T →
    0 ≤ η →
      ∫ (t : ℝ) in 0..T, ‖g.sobolevState 1 t‖ ^ 2 ≤ B →
        ∫ (t : ℝ) in 0..T, ‖h.sobolevState 1 t‖ ^ 2 ≤ B →
          ∀ (K : ℕ),
            0 < K →
              (∀ t ∈ Set.Icc 0 T,
                  ‖(SingularClosure.torusSobolevCutoff 0 K) (g.sobolevState 0 t) -
                        (SingularClosure.torusSobolevCutoff 0 K) (h.sobolevState 0 t)‖ ≤
                    η) →
                ∫ (t : ℝ) in 0..T, ‖g.sobolevState 0 t - h.sobolevState 0 t‖ ^ 2 ≤ 24 * (↑K)⁻¹ * B + 3 * T * η ^ 2
TYPE_END

TYPE_BEGIN
@QuasiFeffermanB.energyGalerkin_finite_test_pairing : ∀ {ν : ℝ} {a : SingularClosure.Field} {N : ℕ}
  (g : SingularClosure.GalerkinSolution ν a N) (b : SingularClosure.FourierCoeff),

    SingularClosure.SpectralSolenoidal b →
      ∀ (t : ℝ),
        0 ≤ t →

                  (SingularClosure.laplacian (SingularClosure.finiteSynthesis N b)) +
              SingularClosure.criticalWeakTransport (g.velocity t) (SingularClosure.finiteSynthesis N b)

          (nhds (SingularClosure.physicalEnergy v))

!=! SingularClosure.GalerkinSolution.energy_H0_time_tail
SOURCE = QuasiFeffermanB/EnergyTimeCompactness.lean
SOURCE_SHA256 = f788ad21d6d1da7a2ea4f8ceb15fd55c4a2217ae61a380139953e20ce25aa9a4
AXIOMS = ["Classical.choice", "Quot.sound", "propext"]
TYPE_BEGIN
@SingularClosure.GalerkinSolution.energy_H0_time_tail : ∀ {ν : ℝ} {a : SingularClosure.Field} {N : ℕ}
  (g : SingularClosure.GalerkinSolution ν a N) (T B : ℝ),
  0 ≤ T →
    ∫ (t : ℝ) in 0..T, ‖g.sobolevState 1 t‖ ^ 2 ≤ B →
      ∀ (K : ℕ),
        0 < K →
          ∫ (t : ℝ) in 0..T, ‖g.sobolevState 0 t - (SingularClosure.torusSobolevCutoff 0 K) (g.sobolevState 0 t)‖ ^ 2 ≤
            4 * (↑K)⁻¹ * B
TYPE_END
